- **integration-planner**: Use after the Design export is staged at `.ui-workflow/export/` and before `design-port` (Scenario C) or `integration-wire` (Scenario B). Scenario C compares, per Screen, the data the design displays and the actions it offers with what the existing backend provides; Scenario B diffs API calls and auth. Writes `.ui-workflow/integration-plan.md` with a PROCEED / PROCEED WITH CAUTION / STOP verdict. Any Blocker means STOP for the Developer. Scenario A skips this step.
